**W2 — ACME/SNI certificate lifecycle (SF-34; SF-33 SNI slice), 2026-11-10, per ADR-007 decision 3 / ADR-009 gated listeners:**

- Implementation: new package `internal/services/certmanager` — `certmanager.go` (Manager: dynamic HostPolicy over the hot-reloading allowlist; `selectCertificate` SNI selection — static cert covering the name → ACME map → static fallback (SF-33 posture), fail-closed when no static cert exists; `MailListenerTLSConfig` no HTTP ALPN / `WebListenerTLSConfig` h2+http1.1+`acme.ALPNProto`; `HTTPChallenge` mounting autocert's HTTP-01 responder in front of the existing HTTP server; `SubmissionTLSGetCert` for the STARTTLS handshake; `CertSolver` DNS-01 seam — deliberately un-wired, see the SF-34 record) and `blobcache.go` (`BlobCache` autocert.Cache over `internal/storage/blob`, keys sanitized to `[A-Za-z0-9._-]` under `certs/acme/`, ErrCacheMiss contract). Config: `internal/config/config.go` AcmeConfig + `newAcmeConfig` + `validateACME` (enabled ⇒ blob store required; HTTPS port range; fallback pair completeness; positive sync interval), `POSTA_SUBMISSION_TLS_MODE=starttls` and the mail-protocol implicit-TLS check now also accept the ACME map; `cmd/posta/server.go` wiring: manager built after blob init (fail-open to static certs on construction error), host source = ownership-verified domains ∪ `POSTA_ACME_DOMAINS` (`newACMEHostSource`), SF-110 hot-reload loop (`StartHostSync`, cancelled in `shutdownServer`), HTTP-01 via `app.UseMiddleware`, webmail HTTPS via `app.With(okapi.WithTLSServer(...))` on `POSTA_ACME_HTTPS_PORT` (default 0 = plain HTTP only), listener signatures extended (`startIMAPServer`/`startPOP3Server`/`startSubmissionServer` — a nil manager preserves the pre-W2 static-cert behavior exactly); `internal/services/mta/submission.go` SubmissionConfig.TLSGetCertificate seam (files win when set). No routes (`internal/routes/*` untouched — the challenge rides a middleware, so the route contract has no diff), no migration (certs live in the blob store).
- Named tests (all in `internal/services/certmanager` + `internal/config/acme_test.go`, all PASS under `-race` 2026-11-10): TestBlobCacheRoundTrip, TestBlobCacheMissingKeyIsCacheMiss, TestBlobCacheKeySanitization, TestSNISelectionMapsPerDomainCerts (7-case matrix incl. unknown-SNI fallback and case/trailing-dot normalization), TestSNISelectionPrefersStaticCertOverACME, TestSNISelectionFailsClosedWithoutBaseCert, TestWebListenerTLSConfigOffersACMEALPN, TestHostAllowlistHotReload, TestRefreshHostsPullsFromSource, TestStartHostSyncStopsOnContextCancel, TestHTTPChallengeMiddlewareRouting (403 disallowed host / 404 unknown token / app fall-through), TestNewRequiresCache, TestCertCoversHostWildcard, TestStaticCertParsesLeafCoverage, and **TestACMEIssuanceAgainstLocalServer — strictly env-gated** (POSTA_TEST_ACME_DIRECTORY + POSTA_TEST_ACME_DOMAIN; `t.Skip` without them, no faked pass; full issuance→blob-cache flow against a local ACME server, mounted HTTP-01 responder on :80); config: TestACMEDisabledByDefault, TestACMEEnvOverrides, TestACMEValidationRequiresBlobStore, TestACMEValidationDisabledIsInert, TestACMEValidationHTTPSAndSyncMatrix.
- Checks as run 2026-11-10 (dockerized golang:1.27, `GOFLAGS=-p=2`): `go build ./internal/services/certmanager/... ./internal/config/... ./internal/services/mta/... ./internal/services/imap/... ./internal/services/pop3/... ./internal/storage/...` → OK; `go test -count=1 -race ./internal/services/certmanager/` → ok 1.311s; `go test -count=1 -race ./internal/services/certmanager/ ./internal/config/` → both ok (config 1.577s). The tree-wide named battery (`go build ./... && go test -count=1 -race ./internal/...`) could not pass at close time: `internal/services/dnsmanager/service.go:132` (W1's in-flight file, `DriftAlerter` vs `*audit.Logger` type error) and `internal/handlers/ip_pool_handler.go:269` (another wave's in-flight file, `undefined: c`) held the tree red — reported per the honesty rules, not fixed by this wave. Frontend checks were run (type-check exit 0; vitest all green — no frontend surface belongs to this wave, the suites are shared-tree).
- Recorded deviations / honest limitations: (1) **DNS-01 is not wired** — autocert exposes no custom-solver hook; the CertSolver seam is reserved and the two activation routes are documented in the package doc (custom acme.Client loop, or the ADR-007-documented Lego upgrade path). (2) Submission 587 is STARTTLS in this tree (RFC 8314); the SNI map serves its STARTTLS handshake — **no implicit-TLS submission port was added** (implicit TLS on 465 is out of scope). (3) The webmail HTTPS listener is a secondary okapi TLS server — the plain-HTTP listener keeps serving the API + the HTTP-01 responder; mapping 443 to `POSTA_ACME_HTTPS_PORT` enables TLS-ALPN-01 on it. (4) Issuance is inline per autocert (a handshake for an unlisted-but-allowlisted domain triggers issuance synchronously, standard autocert behavior). (5) The ACME manager construction fails open to static certificates (logged) so a bad ACME config never takes the TLS listeners down. (6) `docs/docs/operations/deployment.md` + `compose.yml` ACME env names were landed by W6 against this wave's config and match (POSTA_ACME_ENABLED/EMAIL/DOMAINS verified against `newAcmeConfig`).

**W3 — IP pools, warm-up, reputation (SF-07 completion), 2026-11-11, per ADR-007 decision 7:**

- Implementation: additive schema — `internal/models/ip_pool.go` (`IPPool` workspace-or-shared scope, `IPPoolMember` enabled/`PTRName` with the (pool_id, ip) unique key, `IPWarmupSchedule` geometric ramp anchored at `StartedAt`, `IPReputationRollup` per (workspace, ip, domain, day) unique key with transport counters on IP rows and recipient-side events on the domain-wide IP='' row) + `models.Domain.PoolID` (`internal/models/domain.go`) + `models.MTADeliveryAttempt.SourceIP` (`internal/models/mta_delivery_attempt.go`); all registered additive in `internal/storage/migration/migration.go` (no upgrade step needed — new tables/columns with defaults, the OB-1 `mta_delivery_attempts` precedent). Pool selection: `internal/services/mta/deliver.go` `DeliverySpec.PoolIPs`/`Warmup` + `selectBindIP` (atomic round-robin over enabled members, blank-entry skipping, static-bind-IP fallback) + `attemptRow` stamping `SourceIP` on every attempt row; `internal/worker/handler.go` `deliverySpec` resolves the domain's pool members and active schedule (`SetIPPoolRepo` seam; nil keeps byte-for-byte OB-1 behavior), wired in `cmd/posta/worker.go` + `cmd/posta/server.go`. Warm-up math: `internal/services/warmup/warmup.go` (shared leaf package — DayIndex/DailyCap/HourlyCap, multiplier<=0 = flat, floor 1 msg/day, cap 0 after the ramp) re-exported by `internal/services/mta/policy_warmup.go` with `EffectiveHourlyBudget` (cap binds even unlimited domains; policy budget stands after completion); enforcement rides the existing budget gate in `Deliver` (exhaustion defers 450 with a text naming `warm-up cap N/day`). Reputation: `internal/services/reputation/reputation.go` — `RawSource` (gorm: `mta_delivery_attempts` join emails for the sender domain via the shared `repositories.SenderDomainExpr`, `email_events` sent/bounced/complained likewise), pure `AggregateDay`/`aggregateWindow` bucketing by UTC day, idempotent `UpsertRollups` (unique-key overwrite, never double-counts), `DomainReputationReport` (per-IP transport outcomes + domain-wide event join, rates in percent, good/warning/poor/unknown grades at documented thresholds, per-day trend, honest `LimitationNote`), `RefreshDomainDay` (today refreshed on the R-07 read; history owned by the cron), pool/warm-up views. R-07 integration: `internal/services/domain/health.go` `ReputationReader` seam + `HealthReport.Reputation/Pool/Warmup` (omitempty — absent reader preserves the report shape exactly); `internal/handlers/domain_health_handler.go` pass-through setter. Cron: `internal/cron/jobs/ip_reputation_rollup.go` daily 02:15 UTC, previous two days (idempotent), registered in `cmd/posta/server.go`. API: `internal/routes/ip_pool_routes.go` (own opsGroup + own adminGroup, the securityRoutes pattern) — 12 routes: GET/POST/DELETE `/workspaces/current/ip-pools[/{poolId}]`, POST/DELETE `.../members/{memberId}`, PUT `/domains/{id}/pool` (assignment visibility validated: own or shared only, foreign pools 404), PUT `/domains/{id}/warmup`, GET `/domains/{id}/reputation`, admin GET/POST `/admin/ip-pools`, admin member add/remove, admin GET `/admin/ip-reputation?ip=` (per-(workspace, domain) aggregates with the same rate/grade math); `internal/handlers/ip_pool_handler.go` (ownsResource/requireEdit gates, audit events `domain.ip_pool.*`/`domain.pool.assigned`/`domain.warmup.set`/`admin.ip_pool.*`); `internal/routes/routes.go` (handler field + `repSvc` wiring + two Register calls); route contract regenerated (`docs/implementation/api_routes.txt`, 12 new lines). Frontend: `web/src/api/types.ts` (+9 interfaces), `web/src/api/domains.ts` (+10 client methods), `web/src/views/domains/Domains.vue` (Sending IPs & warm-up panel: pool select incl. no-pool, member add/remove/delete, pool create, warm-up start-volume/multiplier/days form with live resolved caps from the health report; per-IP reputation rows with grade badges + limitation note inside the health panel).
- Named tests (all PASS under `-race` 2026-11-11): `internal/services/mta/policy_warmup_test.go` — TestWarmupDayIndexAcrossDays (day walk + anchor clamp + nil), TestWarmupDailyCapAcrossDays (geometric ramp day-by-day, expiry, flat/negative multiplier, 1-msg floor, zero-day ramp), TestWarmupHourlyCapSpreadsDaily (ceil-spread, post-ramp 0, floor 1), TestEffectiveHourlyBudgetFoldsWarmupCap (unlimited capped, tighter policy wins, looser loses, post-ramp untouched). `internal/services/mta/deliver_pool_test.go` — TestExecutorSelectsPoolIPRoundRobin (blank-entry skip), TestExecutorFallsBackToStaticBindIP (all-blank pool / no pool / nothing), TestDeliverDialsFromPoolMembersAndStampsSourceIP (real in-process MX; pool members 127.0.0.2/127.0.0.3 — bindable on Linux; rotation proven on the attempt rows' SourceIP; IP stays the remote MX), TestPoolSelectionIsolatesDomains (two domains' pools never borrow sources), TestDeliverWarmupCapDefersWithoutMX (cap 1/day -> second delivery defers 450 POLICY without contacting the MX; deferred row names `warm-up cap 1/day`), TestDeliverWarmupCapExpiresAfterRampDays. `internal/services/reputation/reputation_test.go` — TestAggregateDayRollupMath (outcome counters, domain normalization, workspace attribution, event row shape), TestAggregateDayBucketingAndNormalization (out-of-window rows dropped), TestReputationReportRatesTrendAndGrade (rates 0.6/0.2/6.5/1.0 pinned, grade boundary, two-point trend order, limitation note), TestReputationGradeThresholds (every threshold boundary), TestReputationIsolationAcrossWorkspaces (ws4's permanent failure never enters ws3's report), TestServiceRollupAndReportRoundTrip (RollupDay -> store -> scoped report end-to-end over fakes). `internal/storage/repositories/ip_pool_repo_test.go` — TestIPPoolRepoVisiblePoolsScopeFilter (DryRun: the visibility conjunction bound to the caller), TestIPPoolRepoRollupsForDomainScopeFilter (workspace + day-window bounds; nil scope matches nothing), TestIPPoolRepoDeletePoolCountsDomainRefs (the ErrPoolHasDomains guard query). Existing suites re-verified: handlers fake drivers extended with the additive `domains.pool_id` column (`dmarc_handler_test.go`, `domain_signing_handler_test.go`, `internal/cron/jobs/dkim_retire_test.go` — GORM Save() emits it in every SET list).
- Checks as run 2026-11-11 (dockerized golang:1.27, `GOFLAGS=-p=2`, volumes `posta-gomod`/`posta-gocache`): `go build ./...` -> exit 0 (full tree incl. `./cmd/...`); package batteries under `-race` -> `internal/services/mta`, `internal/services/reputation`, `internal/services/domain`, `internal/storage/repositories`, `internal/cron/jobs`, `internal/worker` all `ok`; `go test ./internal/routes/ -run TestAPIRouteContract -update` -> ok, golden diff = the 12 new route lines (verified by grep). The full named battery `go build ./... && go test -count=1 -race ./internal/...` result is recorded in this wave's final report (executed against the shared tree with the other OB-6 waves' files present). Frontend: `npm --prefix web run type-check` exit 0; `npm --prefix web run test` 23 files / 122 tests passed.
- Recorded deviations / honest limitations: (1) reputation transport counters require `mta_delivery_attempts` (direct mode only) — relay-mode mail has no attempt rows, so its transport side is invisible to the surface (stated in `LimitationNoteText` on every report); recipient events cover every mode. (2) Warm-up enforces a spread hourly cap (daily/24, ceil), not a per-message throttle. (3) One selection strategy (`round_robin`); weighting/geo strategies are additive column values. (4) `RefreshDomainDay` refreshes today's rollup on the workspace health read (bounded to the one domain; idempotent upsert) — history belongs to the daily cron; the admin `/admin/ip-reputation` reads stored rollups only. (5) The budget keeper stays per-process (documented on `BudgetKeeper` since OB-1) — an N-worker deployment enforces roughly N times the cap; warm-up inherits that documented limitation. (6) Blacklist alerts + abuse-response runbook (RF-B123 AC 3's remainder) and metrics-package signals (RF-B123 AC 4's "metrics" half) are NOT in this wave and remain open on RF-B123 — SF-07's completion is scoped to the register's own wording (source-IP pools), not to all of RF-B123.

## OB-6 wave evidence (2026-11-11 — wave W4: MTA-STS + TLS-RPT, SF-29/31, per ADR-007 §5)

Scope of this wave: MTA-STS policy hosting for our domains (TXT/Web-Host guidance + HTTPS policy path on the existing HTTP server, mode enforce/none per domain with caching) and outbound enforcement in the MTA session runner; TLS-RPT inbound report ingest per the ADR-002 pattern (dedicated RCPT allowlist rua address, gzip/size caps, idempotent `tls_rpt_reports` storage, read surface) plus outbound report generation for our own delivery failure aggregates (scheduled task, SF-104 slice).

**Implementation map:**

- MTA-STS client + enforcement: `internal/services/mta/mtasts.go` (`STSRecordID`, `ParseSTSPolicy`, `RenderSTSPolicy`, `MXAllowed`, `PolicyClient` TTL cache with negative caching + `Invalidate`, `Enforcer` over the small `STSEnforcement` interface, process-wide `SetDefaultSTS`/`DefaultSTS` seam); session runner hook `internal/services/mta/session.go` (`SessionOptions.STS`, pre-dial host allowlist + STARTTLS-required under mode: enforce, recipient domain re-derived from the validated recipients; nil hook + nil default = byte-identical pre-W4 behavior). **deliver.go untouched** (the pool wave owns it this round): the ask's "single integration line" turned out unnecessary because the runner falls back to the process-wide hook when `SessionOptions.STS` is nil — the executor's untouched opts literal inherits enforcement from `newMTAExecutor`'s `mta.SetDefaultSTS(mta.NewEnforcer(mta.NewPolicyClient()))` (`cmd/posta/server.go`; the same builder serves `cmd/posta/worker.go`). The relay path is unaffected by construction (`internal/services/email` never calls `runSession`).
- MTA-STS hosting: `internal/handlers/mta_sts_policy_handler.go` (Host-header policy document, live MX via injected `lookupMX` + 60s cache, 404 posture documented inline), route `internal/routes/mta_sts_routes.go` gated by `POSTA_MTA_STS_ENABLED` (config `internal/config/config.go` `MTASTSEnabled`, default false per ADR-009 §1), contract test + golden updated. Per-domain mode: `models.Domain.MTASTSMode` (additive column, `column:mta_sts_mode` pinned — gorm's default naming collapses the acronym to `mtasts_mode`), handler `SetMTASTSMode` (requireEdit/ownsResource/verified-domain fail-closed/audited), route `PATCH /domains/{id}/mta-sts`. TXT/Web-Host guidance: `internal/services/domain/verifier.go` `RequiredRecords` gains `tls_rpt` (v=TLSRPTv1; rua=mailto:tlsrpt@<domain>) and `mta_sts` (A record for mta-sts.<domain>) records.
- TLS-RPT inbound: `internal/services/inbound/tlsrpt.go` (`TlsRptAllowlist`, `EnableTLSRPT`/`SetTLSRPTAddress` wiring seams, `ResolveTLSRPTRecipient` nil-safe, `ingestSystemTLSRPT` durable-first system ingest with sync-parse fallback when no producer is wired, processor mirroring dmarc.go with shared bounded-read helpers), receiver branch `internal/services/inbound/smtp_server.go` Rcpt, ingest-path branch + two parse hooks `internal/services/inbound/service.go` (mirroring the DMARC hook points exactly), parse-service wiring `cmd/posta/worker.go` (`parseSvc.EnableTLSRPT`), receiver wiring `cmd/posta/server.go` (`attachTLSRPT` + `backend.SetTLSRPT`).
- TLS-RPT shared package: `internal/services/tlsrpt` — `tlsrpt.go` (`ParseTLSRPTRecord`, `ParseTLSRPTReport`, `OutboundReport`, `OutboundReportID`, `LookupTXT` seam), `sender.go` (`Sender`/`SenderFunc`, `GzipAttachment`, `ReportText`), `aggregate.go` (`Aggregator`: bounded attempt scan + typed email loads, single-recipient-domain attribution, outcome classification, deterministic ordering).
- TLS-RPT outbound: `internal/cron/jobs/tlsrpt_report.go` (daily 06:35 UTC pass; inert without identity; rua only from live DNS; send failure logged not fatal), registered in `cmd/posta/server.go` right after `InitRoutes` (needs the email service for the sender adapter `newTLSRPTSender`), gated on `cfg.MTASTSOrgDomain` (`POSTA_MTA_STS_ORG`, default empty = never sends).
- Read surface: `internal/storage/repositories/tls_rpt_repo.go` (`Summary` + `Recent`, every query through the workspace-scoping helper), `internal/handlers/tls_rpt_handler.go` (owner-scoped, 404 for foreign domains), route `GET /domains/{id}/tls-rpt`.
- Migration: `internal/models/tls_rpt.go` (`TLSRPTReport`, unique index `(user_id, report_id, policy_domain)`) + `models.Domain.MTASTSMode`, both registered additively in `internal/storage/migration/migration.go` (new table + new column with defaults — the OB-1/OB-2 additive precedent; no destructive change).

**Named tests (all PASS under `-race` unless noted):**

- `internal/services/mta/mtasts_test.go` — TestSTSRecordIDParse, TestParseSTSPolicyValidEnforce, TestParseSTSPolicyValidNoneNoMX, TestParseSTSPolicyRejectsMalformed (15 malformed fixtures), TestParseSTSPolicyRejectsOversize, TestRenderSTSPolicyShape (+parse round-trip), TestRenderSTSPolicyRefusesInvalid, TestMXAllowed (12-case matrix), TestPolicyClientCachesPositive (hit/expiry/refresh), TestPolicyClientCachesNegative (+Invalidate), TestPolicyClientFallsOpenOnFetchFailure, TestPolicyClientIgnoresForeignTXT, TestEnforcerSessionRule, TestSetDefaultSTSWiring, TestFetchSTSPolicyRejectsNonText, TestFetchSTSPolicyHappyPath, TestMXMatchFromSessionRuleNilSafety.
- `internal/services/mta/session_sts_test.go` — TestSessionSTSRefusesHostOutsidePolicy (421 STS, pre-dial refusal), TestSessionSTSEnforceRequiresSTARTTLS, TestSessionSTSEnforceDeliversViaPolicyHostWithTLS, TestSessionSTSTestingModeDoesNotBlock, TestSessionSTSNoneModeIsNoop, TestSessionSTSOffByDefault, TestSessionSTSDefaultCoversNilOption (the executor-wiring contract), TestSessionSTSExplicitHookEnforces, TestSessionSTSEnforcementNeverTouchesSmarthostSessions.
- `internal/services/tlsrpt/tlsrpt_test.go` — TestParseTLSRPTRecord, TestParseTLSRPTReportValid, TestParseTLSRPTReportEmptyPoliciesIsValid, TestParseTLSRPTReportRejects (7 fixtures), TestOutboundReportShapeAndDeterminism (+ parser round-trip), TestOutboundReportRefusesEmptyWindow, TestOutboundReportIDDeterministic, TestRecipientDomainsDerivation, TestChunkIDs.
- `internal/services/tlsrpt/aggregate_test.go` — TestAggregatorAttributionAndClassification (delivered→successful; reached-and-failed→failed; DNS/budget/refusal→no session; multi-domain email skipped; out-of-window excluded), TestAggregatorTruncatesAtCap, TestAggregatorNeedsDB.
- `internal/services/inbound/tls_rpt_ingest_test.go` — TestTlsRptAllowlistGrammar, TestTLSRPTStoreIsIdempotent, TestTLSRPTStoreCarriesPolicyFacts (incl. workspace/domain scoping columns), TestTLSRPTProcessGzipIdempotentAndBounded (gzip happy path, idempotent double-process, gzip bomb skipped not fatal, non-report silent miss), TestTLSRPTResolveRecipient (nil-safety), TestTLSRPTWindowFromReport.
- `internal/cron/jobs/tlsrpt_report_test.go` — TestTLSRPTReportJobSendsToPublishedRuas (document round-trips, report-id keyed on window+domain, quiet domain skipped), TestTLSRPTReportJobInertWithoutIdentity, TestTLSRPTReportJobQuietWindowSendsNothing, TestTLSRPTReportJobSendFailureIsLoggedNotFatal.
- `internal/handlers/tls_rpt_handler_test.go` — TestMTASTSPolicyDocumentShape (content-type, Cache-Control, field lines, parser round-trip), TestMTASTSPolicyRefusesWhenNotOptedIn, TestMTASTSPolicyRefusesForeignHostAndMissingDomain (dashboard host / unknown / unverified), TestMTASTSPolicyRefusesWithoutResolvableMX, TestTLSRPTGetSummaryAndRecent (incl. foreign-workspace row exclusion), TestTLSRPTGetForeignDomainNotFound, TestTLSRPTGetEmptySummary.

**Checks executed 2026-11-11 (dockerized golang:1.27, volumes `posta-gomod`/`posta-gocache`, `GOFLAGS=-p=2`):**

- `go test -race -count=1 -run 'TestSTS|TestParseSTSPolicy|TestRenderSTSPolicy|TestMXAllowed|TestPolicyClient|TestEnforcerSessionRule|TestSetDefaultSTS|TestFetchSTSPolicy|TestSessionSTS|TestMXMatch' ./internal/services/mta/` → ok.
- `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/services/tlsrpt/` → ok; `go test -race -count=1 -run 'TestTLSRPT|TestTlsRpt' ./internal/services/inbound/` → ok; `go test -race -count=1 -run 'TestTLSRPTReportJob' ./internal/cron/jobs/` → ok; `go test -race -count=1 -run 'TestMTASTSPolicy|TestTLSRPTGet' ./internal/handlers/` → ok.
- Package battery `go test -race -count=1 ./internal/services/mta/ ./internal/services/tlsrpt/ ./internal/services/domain/ ./internal/cron/jobs/ ./internal/services/inbound/ ./internal/models/` → all ok. (One intermediate run showed `TestDeliverDialsFromPoolMembersAndStampsSourceIP` in the POOL wave's `deliver_pool_test.go` failing to bind TEST-NET source address 192.0.2.11 — environment-dependent, not this wave's file; it passed in the full battery runs and was not touched by this wave.)
- `go test ./internal/routes/ -run TestAPIRouteContract -update` → ok (3 new lines: GET `/.well-known/mta-sts.txt`, PATCH `/api/v1/workspaces/current/domains/{id}/mta-sts`, GET `/api/v1/workspaces/current/domains/{id}/tls-rpt`); contract test re-run without `-update` → ok.
- Named battery `docker run --rm -m 2500m -v <repo>:/app -v posta-gomod:/go/pkg/mod -v posta-gocache:/root/.cache/go-build -w /app -e GOFLAGS=-p=2 golang:1.27 sh -c "go build ./... && go test -count=1 -race ./internal/..."` → **exit 0, 56 packages ok, 0 FAIL** (final full run 2026-11-11). The first full run exited 1 with exactly two failures, both this wave's own forced adaptation: the additive `domains.mta_sts_mode` column reaches every GORM `Save()` SET list, and the shared fake-driver `domains` tables (`internal/handlers/dmarc_handler_test.go`, `internal/handlers/domain_signing_handler_test.go`, `internal/cron/jobs/dkim_retire_test.go`) needed the column added (the OB-6 W3 `domains.pool_id` precedent); after that fix both packages re-ran green under `-race` and the second full run passed clean. A first-run-only artifact — one `TestHostAllowlistHotReload` delta-ordering flake in the ACME wave's `internal/services/certmanager` — did not reproduce in either later run and is not this wave's code; reported for the owning wave.
- Frontend: `npm --prefix web run type-check` → exit 0; `npm --prefix web run test` → 23 files / 122 tests passed (no frontend surface belongs to this wave; the suites cover the shared tree incl. the route-contract guard admin.spec.ts).

**Recorded deviations / honest limitations:**

1. **Per-attempt TLS outcomes are not recorded** — `mta_delivery_attempts` has no per-attempt TLS column this round (writing it needs the `attemptRow`/`classifySession` path in deliver.go, owned by the pool wave). Consequence: TLS-RPT outbound counts are SMTP-session-scoped (delivered = successful session, reached-and-failed = failed session), documented verbatim on `tlsrpt.OutboundReport` and in the SF-31 record — never silently presented as TLS handshake outcomes. The enforcement hook observes TLS state per session (STARTTLS required/refused under enforce) but does not persist it.
2. **STS policy-fetch errors are not yet reported via TLS-RPT** (`sts-policy-fetch-error` detail type) — the same per-attempt substrate gap; the session runner surfaces fetch failure as fall-open (RFC 8461 §6.1) by design.
3. Outbound report attribution skips emails with more than one recipient domain (the attempt row does not carry the recipient domain; mis-attribution is never guessed) — logged per skip, documented on the Aggregator.
4. The MTA-STS policy endpoint serves HTTP behind the operator's reverse proxy; TLS for `mta-sts.<domain>` terminates at that proxy (ADR-009 §2 posture, documented on the handler). `POSTA_MTA_STS_ENABLED` gates the path; the outbound enforcement half is always armed (it only tightens behavior for remote domains that publish enforce policies themselves and falls open on discovery failure per RFC 8461 §6.1).
5. Dashboard surface (mode toggle, TLS-RPT view) not in this wave — the API surface is complete and contract-pinned; the Vue area remains open work.
6. `failure-details` in outbound reports are omitted this slice (no honest `sending-mta-ip` until the SourceIP join is threaded through the aggregator; the pool wave's `SourceIP` column makes this a small follow-up).

## OB-6 wave evidence (2026-11-11 — wave W5: observability completion, per ADR-009 + ADR-008 event substrate)

Scope of this wave: the SF-93 OTLP push exporter slice, the SF-96 metric-alert engine, GAP-51 (error-monitoring hook), GAP-52 (sample Grafana dashboard). Registry movement: SF-93 PARTIAL → COMPLETE, SF-96 PLANNED → COMPLETE, CA-07 COMPLETE-for-metrics (trace residual recorded), RF-F097 To-dos → Done (registry), RF-F106 minted (registry, Done).

**SF-93 — OTLP push exporter (landed, zero new dependency):**

- Implementation: `internal/observability/otlp.go` — gathers `prometheus.DefaultGatherer` (exactly what `/metrics` exposes), hand-encodes the OTLP `ExportMetricsServiceRequest` protobuf wire format with the standard library only (`encoding/binary`; `pbWriter`: varint/64-bit/length-delimited + packed repeated scalars), POSTs to `POSTA_OTLP_ENDPOINT` + `/v1/metrics` with `Content-Type: application/x-protobuf`. **Dependency choice recorded for the ADR-009 ledger:** `go.opentelemetry.io/otel/exporters/otlp` was NOT added — it drags the OTel SDK + proto module tree for a job that re-encodes families the registry already exposes; `prometheus/client_model` (already in the module graph via client_golang) becomes a direct import; go.mod gains no new module. Mapping: counters→Sum (monotonic, cumulative), gauges/untyped→Gauge, histograms→Histogram (cumulative; explicit bounds with +Inf implicit; `bucket_counts` carries `len(bounds)+1` CUMULATIVE entries with the +Inf entry = SampleCount — client_golang's DTO omits the +Inf bucket, derived), summaries→Summary (quantiles). Resource attributes `service.name=posta` + `service.version=<build>`; scope `github.com/goposta/posta/internal/observability`. Config: `POSTA_OTLP_ENDPOINT` (EMPTY = OFF — nothing ships by default), `POSTA_OTLP_INTERVAL_SECS` (default 60), `POSTA_OTLP_HEADERS` (`Name=Value,...`; a pair without `=` is refused — a silently dropped auth header would 401 later). Wiring: `cmd/posta/server.go` OnStart (stopped first in `shutdownServer`), `cmd/posta/worker.go` (deferred stop) — push failures log and retry next tick; boot never blocks on the collector.
- Transport trust class (recorded because the non-negotiables bind it): the OTLP endpoint is operator ENV infrastructure — the same trust class as `POSTA_DB_HOST` — so it is deliberately NOT routed through the tenant-webhook SSRF guard (`internal/services/webhook.ValidateWebhookURL`), which exists for URLs less-privileged actors store in the database and which would block the common self-hosted private-network collector. The guard itself is untouched. The ALERT-engine webhook URLs (settings-stored) DO go through the guard — see SF-96.

**SF-96 — metric-alert engine (landed):**

- Rules live in the platform setting `metric_alert_rules` (JSON array): id, name, metric, optional exact-match label conjunction, condition (`gt|gte|lt|lte|eq|neq`), threshold, `duration_seconds` (0 fires on the first observed breach), optional webhook_url + webhook_secret, optional email, enabled. Managed through the existing admin-gated settings API (`PUT /admin/settings`) — NO new routes, so `docs/implementation/api_routes.txt` has no diff and the route contract test passes untouched.
- Evaluation: `internal/cron/jobs/metric_alert.go` (`metric-alert`, `* * * * *`, registered unconditionally in `cmd/posta/server.go` `initCronManager` — inert without rules, the bounce-alert posture) → `internal/services/meticalert/engine.go` `Evaluate`: gather → per-rule breach state machine (first breach stamps `breachSince`; held ≥ duration fires ONCE per episode; clearing resolves). Fire actions in order: (1) `metric_alert.fired` platform event (category system, via `EventRepository.Create` — the ADR-008 event substrate); (2) webhook POST `{event, rule_id, rule_name, metric, labels, value, threshold, condition, duration_seconds, held_seconds, fired_at, resolved}` signed `X-Posta-Signature: sha256=<hex>` via the shared `webhook.SignBody` and sent through `webhook.ValidateWebhookURL` + `webhook.NewGuardedTransport` (destination pinned at dial time) — the SSRF guard REUSED, not weakened, identical to user-webhook delivery; (3) optional email via `notification.Service.Send` + the new `metric_alert` template. Resolve actions: `metric_alert.resolved` event + webhook, no email.
- Tenancy: platform metrics are deployment-wide; rules are platform settings (admin-only writes; workspace users can neither read nor write); events carry no workspace scope. Per ADR-008's checklist this wave adds no new table (the rules JSON rides the existing `settings` table — additive-only trivially satisfied, no migration).

**GAP-51 — error-monitoring hook (landed, default off):**

- `internal/observability/errorhook.go`: `POSTA_ERROR_WEBHOOK_URL` (+ optional `POSTA_ERROR_WEBHOOK_SECRET`) ships panics and fatals as `{"event":"runtime.error","kind":"panic|fatal","message",...,"stack"(16 KiB cap),"timestamp","service":"posta","version"}` — panic path async (the `internal/middlewares/panic_capture.go` middleware, registered first in `internal/routes/routes.go`, reports then RE-PANICS so net/http's per-connection behavior is byte-for-byte unchanged), fatal path synchronous with a 3s cap (called before every `logger.Fatal` in `cmd/posta/server.go`, `worker.go`, `main.go`). Both also record best-effort `runtime.panic`/`runtime.fatal` platform events (category system) so the admin activity timeline keeps the incident even when the webhook target is down; every failure mode is logged, never propagated. HMAC: same `webhook.SignBody` scheme (`X-Posta-Signature: sha256=<hex>`) when a secret is set. The URL/secret are operator ENV (same trust class as above; the tenant-webhook SSRF guard is not applied and not weakened). Original entry formulation (Sentry DSN + `posta_errors_total` family) superseded by the ask's webhook hook — residuals recorded on the BACKLOG RF-F097 entry, not silently dropped.

**GAP-52 — sample Grafana dashboard (landed):**

- `docs/observability/grafana-posta-overview.json` (uid `posta-overview`, schemaVersion 39, `DS_PROMETHEUS` datasource variable): 17 visual panels over 7 rows — HTTP traffic (rate by status, p95/p50 latency, 5xx ratio), email delivery (sent/failed rate, queue size + retries), bounces/suppressions, webhooks (deliveries by status, duration p95, SF-90 drops), inbound mail (received by source / forwarded vs failed, rejects by reason, ingest p95 + bytes), queue health (dead-letter depth by queue, active workers), campaigns/automations. `docs/observability/README.md` documents the metric inventory, the OTLP config, the alert-rule format with a payload example, the hook payload, and dashboard import.
- Guard test: `internal/observability/dashboard_test.go` — TestGrafanaDashboardJSONIsValid parses the committed JSON and resolves EVERY `posta_*` token in every panel expression against the live default gatherer (Vec families seeded, histograms observed) incl. the `_bucket/_sum/_count` histogram series names; TestGrafanaDashboardCoversCoreFamilies requires the traffic/delivery/queue/inbound groups — a renamed family without a dashboard update now fails the build.

**Named tests (all PASS under `-race`, 2026-11-11):** `internal/observability`: TestOTLPExportPayloadShape, TestOTLPExporterDisabledWithoutEndpoint, TestOTLPExporterRejectsBadEndpoint, TestOTLPExportNon2xxIsError, TestParseOTLPHeaders, TestErrorHookEmitsPanicEvent, TestErrorHookEmitsFatalEventSynchronously, TestErrorHookStoreFailureDoesNotBlockDelivery, TestErrorHookDisabledWithoutURL, TestCaptureGlobalFatalWithoutHookIsNoOp, TestGrafanaDashboardJSONIsValid, TestGrafanaDashboardCoversCoreFamilies. `internal/services/meticalert`: TestAlertMatrixConditions (13-case fire/no-fire table), TestAlertDurationGate, TestAlertLabelFilterMismatch, TestAlertCounterGaugeAndAbsentMetrics, TestAlertSkippedHistogramMetric, TestAlertDisabledAndRemovedRules, TestAlertFireActionsEventWebhookEmail, TestAlertResolveWebhook, TestAlertInvalidRulesAreReported. `internal/cron/jobs`: TestMetricAlertJobImplementsCronJob, TestMetricAlertJobRunsEngine. `internal/config`: TestObservabilityConfigDefaultsOff.

**Battery:** the ask's named battery — `docker run --rm -m 2500m -v C:/Users/Ibrahim/Desktop/ReForwarder:/app -v posta-gomod:/go/pkg/mod -v posta-gocache:/root/.cache/go-build -w /app -e GOFLAGS=-p=2 golang:1.27 sh -c "go build ./... && go test -count=1 -race ./internal/..."` (with the `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1` prefix Git Bash requires for `-w /app`) — ran `go build ./...` OK and 63 packages `ok` under `-race`, including every package this wave touched (`internal/observability ok 5.354s`, `internal/services/meticalert ok 1.377s`, `internal/cron ok 1.385s`, `internal/cron/jobs ok 4.229s`, `internal/metrics ok 1.947s`, `internal/middlewares ok 2.875s`, `internal/services/notification ok 1.576s`, `internal/services/webhook ok 15.212s`, `internal/routes ok 4.026s`, `internal/config ok 2.805s`), with ONE failure: `internal/handlers [build failed]` — `vet: open internal/handlers/zz_scratch_test.go: no such file or directory`: another wave deleted its scratch test file from the shared working tree while the battery was mid-run (the file does not exist at close; that package holds none of this wave's code). Settled-tree re-run of exactly that package: 2 failures / rest green — `TestDomainHealthDiagnosePersistsAndAudits` + `TestDeliveryModeSwitchToDirect`, both `unknown column "mta_sts_mode"`/500 on the domain UPDATE path: the MTA-STS wave's uncommitted `internal/models/domain.go:83` (`MTASTSMode`) outrunning the package's fake-driver test schema — none of them in this wave's code paths. Reported as run per the honesty rules. Frontend: `npm --prefix web run type-check` exit 0; `npm --prefix web run test` 121/122 — the single failure is `src/api/admin.spec.ts` flagging 8 unknown endpoints, ALL from another wave's uncommitted `web/src/api/domains.ts` (`/workspaces/current/ip-pools*`, `.../warmup`, `.../reputation`) whose backend routes are not yet registered; this wave touched no frontend files and no routes.

**Deviations / honest limitations:** alert breach-duration state is in-memory (a restart restarts a rule's duration timer — documented; a persisted breach ledger was judged not worth a new table for a minute-granularity evaluator); histogram/summary families are not alertable (skipped with a warning, tested); OTLP points carry `start_time_unix_nano=0` (Prometheus gather has no series start); trace export is NOT adopted — there is no tracing instrumentation to export (CA-07 records the residual); the hook covers the HTTP panic path + cmd/posta fatal sites, while SMTP/IMAP/POP3 session-level `recover()` sites keep local logging (recorded residual).

## OB-6 wave evidence (2026-11-11 — wave W1: DNS automation + drift, SF-61, per ADR-007 decision 1)

The W1 wave landed with its record edits but without an evidence section here; the block below books it (scope and checks as reported by the wave; file existence and the cited code points spot-verified at the 2026-11-11 close-out, citations below):

- **Implementation:** new package `internal/services/dnsmanager` — `provider.go` (`DNSProvider` interface with exactly PublishRecordSet/ReadRecords/DeleteRecord, `internal/services/dnsmanager/provider.go:131`; `DNSRecordSpec`, typed sentinel errors `ErrUnauthorized/ErrZoneNotFound/ErrRateLimited/ErrProviderUnavailable/ErrNotConnected/ErrDomainNotVerified/ErrMailHostMissing`, and `*PublishError` carrying the applied-record prefix); adapters `cloudflare.go` (REST v4, zone resolution by exact name, paginated reads, per-record upsert, quote-tolerant TXT comparison), `route53.go` (local SigV4 signing — zero new dependencies, ADR-007's dependency posture — one atomic ChangeBatch per publish with sibling-value preservation, exact-value DELETE), `digitalocean.go` (API v2, @-apex relative names); **all three validate access with a read before any write** — a mis-scoped credential fails typed with zero writes (ADR-007 decision 2). Planner `planner.go`: verification/SPF/DKIM/DMARC always (managed R-02 signing-key TXT via the email seam; legacy upstream CNAME otherwise), MX + return-path only for ownership-verified domains with the explicit `POSTA_DNS_MAIL_ROUTING` opt-in (`internal/config/config.go:762`); publish is refused for unverified domains (ADR-008 fail-closed). Service `service.go`: connect validates read-only then stores encrypted (AES-256-GCM via the crypto package, SF-78), one connection per domain, per-domain revocation; `PublishDomainRecords` = plan → publish → always the existing R-07 verification flow with the fresh check booleans persisted and the publication baseline recorded on `dns_publication_state`; `CheckDrift` diffs live provider records against the baseline (missing/changed findings; unrelated zone records are not drift), persists ok/drift/unknown, and fires `domain.dns_drift_detected`/`domain.dns_drift_resolved` audit alerts on transitions only (`internal/services/dnsmanager/service.go:591,601`) — a provider outage never masquerades as drift. Storage: `models.DNSProviderCredential` + `models.DNSPublicationState` (additive, `internal/storage/migration/migration.go`; unique domain_id, workspace ownership per ADR-008 §1) behind the gorm repository `internal/storage/repositories/dns_provider_repo.go` (parameterized queries only). Drift cron `internal/cron/jobs/dns_drift_check.go` (hourly at :23; per-domain failures never abort the sweep), registered only when `POSTA_DNS_AUTOMATION_ENABLED` && cronManager != nil (default off, ADR-009 §8). API (minimal-additive, workspace-scoped): POST/GET/DELETE `/domains/{id}/dns-provider`, POST `/domains/{id}/dns-records/publish`, GET `/domains/{id}/dns-drift` in `internal/routes/workspace_resource_routes.go` — ownsResource tenant scoping (foreign domain = 404), requireEdit gating, typed-error HTTP mapping (401/404/409/429/502), audited connect/revoke/publish; `DashboardFeatures.dns_automation` UI gate. Route contract regenerated (`docs/implementation/api_routes.txt:44,177,178,357,358` — the five DNS lines, verified at close-out). Frontend: `web/src/api/domains.ts` + types + the DNS-automation panel in `web/src/views/domains/Domains.vue` (provider connect with provider-specific credential fields, masked fingerprint + revoke, planned records with per-record copy, confirm-gated publish, drift badge + findings; 404/503 flips the panel off for feature-disabled deployments).
- **Named tests (as reported by the wave):** 47 `internal/services/dnsmanager` tests (adapters vs httptest fake API servers for all three providers — upsert/idempotency/no-write-on-rejected-token/typed-error classification/atomic single batch/sibling preservation; planner completeness incl. all six kinds + opt-in + fail-closed gating; masking never leaking the raw token + encrypted-at-rest proof; connect/publish/drift flows; drift detection/resolution/outage/unrelated-records; a constructor-bug-catching routing test), 8 handler tests over the real okapi stack (masking in responses, 404 tenant scoping, 409 unverified publish, typed mapping, revocation, publish happy path), 3 drift-job tests (nil-inertness, multi-domain sweep resilience, connected scan set).
- **Checks (as reported by the wave, 2026-11-11, dockerized golang:1.27, `GOFLAGS=-p=2`, volumes `posta-gomod`/`posta-gocache`):** the named battery `go build ./... && go test -count=1 -race ./internal/...` → exit 0, 56 packages ok, zero FAIL (final run; first attempts failed only on other waves' concurrently-landing files, attributed in the wave report, never fixed by W1); `go test -count=1 -race ./internal/services/dnsmanager/` ok; handler + drift-job focused runs ok; route contract `go test ./internal/routes/ -run TestAPIRouteContract -update` → ok, +21 golden lines (W1's five DNS routes plus the concurrently-registering waves' routes), re-pinned without `-update`; `npm --prefix web run type-check` exit 0; `npm --prefix web run test` 23 files / 122 tests. Not run by W1: `make lint`/`make vet` (not its named checks), ACME/MTA-STS/pools/observability (separate waves). No commit/push (not authorized).

## Resumability

This register is the programme's persistent tracker: after every wave, statuses, evidence, and the tally are updated in place. Any interrupted phase resumes from the register without loss of progress. Programme-level phase tracking lives in `todo.md` Track A and `docs/implementation/ROADMAP_IMPLEMENTATION.md`.
